# AGENTS.md instructions for D:\100XH100

<INSTRUCTIONS>
# Project Agent Rules

## Operating Rule
- Read `AGENTS.md` before project edits.
- Update `memory/CHANGELOG.md` after meaningful code, config, or architecture changes.
- Update `memory/PROMPTS.md` with user prompts that define requirements.
- Store test outputs, logs, and verification notes under `test_results/`.
- Prefer agent-centered code: explicit contracts, deterministic tests, small files, low hidden state.

## Architecture Source
- Primary architecture contract: `ARCHITECTURE_NEED.md`.
- Current Stream 4 implementation rule: use threshold + compact + CUB/fixed-temp sort/reduce + compact; do not use Stream 4 hash-table dedup.

## Implementation Guardrails
- `State128` logical bytes are `v[0..119]`.
- `State128` padding bytes are `v[120..127]`.
- Persistent frontier padding must be zero.
- `FinalResponse` may temporarily store `target_local_idx` in `v[120..123]`.
- `Hash128` is one logical 128-bit key stored as `{lo, hi}`.
- `CandidateMeta` must remain 32 bytes and 32-byte aligned.
- Stream 3 payload id must be original candidate id, not compact index.
- Stream 4 must not apply shard top-k or semantic shard cap.

</INSTRUCTIONS>

def run_depth80_k1_5():
    import os, pathlib, subprocess, shutil
    exe = pathlib.Path('/tmp/molab_sm120_build/build-sm120-finalvalidate/production_runner')
    beam, depth, puzzle = 260_000_000, 80, 992
    roots = []
    for p in ['/mnt/first/beam_history_250m_depth80_k1_5', '/tmp/beam_history_250m_depth80_k1_5']:
        q = pathlib.Path(p)
        try:
            q.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            t = q / '.write_test'
            t.write_text('ok')
            t.unlink()
            roots.append((q, shutil.disk_usage(q).free))
        except Exception as e:
            print(f'history_path_skip={p} reason={type(e).__name__}:{e}', flush=True)
    disk_path, free_bytes = max(roots, key=lambda x: x[1])
    env = os.environ.copy()
    env.update({
        'BEAM_SHARD_COUNT': '64',
        'BEAM_SHARD_CAPACITY_CANDIDATES': '4100096',
        'BEAM_B_MICRO': '4096',
        'BEAM_STREAM1_CONCURRENCY': '2',
        'BEAM_STREAM3_RING_SLOTS': '4',
        'BEAM_STREAM4_BATCH_CANDIDATES': '524288',
        'BEAM_STREAM4_TRIGGER_CANDIDATES': '1048576',
        'BEAM_STREAM4_ACTIVE_SORT_SLOTS': '4',
        'BEAM_SHARD_BUFFER_COUNT': '2',
        'BEAM_GLOBAL_SPILL_CAPACITY': '0',
        'BEAM_HISTORY_MODE': 'static_hybrid',
        'BEAM_HISTORY_RAM_BYTES': str(96 * 1024**3),
        'BEAM_HISTORY_DISK_BYTES': str(192 * 1024**3),
        'BEAM_HISTORY_DISK_PATH': str(disk_path),
        'BEAM_HISTORY_SLOT_COUNT': '3',
        'BEAM_HISTORY_WORKERS': '1',
        'BEAM_SOLVED_NEIGHBORHOOD_RADIUS': '5',
        'BEAM_SOLVED_NEIGHBORHOOD_MAX_ENTRIES': '0',
        'BEAM_STREAM2_SUFFIX_RADIUS': '0',
        'BEAM_DEPTH_LOG_EVERY': '1',
        'BEAM_DEBUG_FINAL_VALIDATE': '0',
        'CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES': '0',
    })
    log_path = pathlib.Path('/tmp/molab_250m_depth80_k1_5_hybrid.log')
    cmd = [str(exe), str(puzzle), str(depth), str(beam)]
    print('RUN_DEPTH80_K1_5_HYBRID_START', flush=True)
    print('cmd=', ' '.join(cmd), flush=True)
    print('cwd= /tmp/molab_beam_100m24/repo', flush=True)
    print('history_disk_path=', disk_path, flush=True)
    print('history_disk_free_gib=', round(free_bytes / 1024**3, 2), flush=True)
    print('history_ram_gib=96 history_disk_gib=192 k1=5 k2=0', flush=True)
    print('log_path=', log_path, flush=True)
    with log_path.open('w', buffering=1) as log:
        proc = subprocess.Popen(cmd, cwd='/tmp/molab_beam_100m24/repo', env=env, stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT, text=True, bufsize=1)
        for line in proc.stdout:
            log.write(line)
            low = line.lower()
            if ('runner_phase=' in line or 'candidate_history_' in line or 'history_' in line or line.startswith('depth ') or 'puzzle_' in line or 'error' in low or 'exception' in low or 'RUN' in line):
                print(line, end='', flush=True)
        rc = proc.wait()
    print(f'RUN_DEPTH80_K1_5_HYBRID_RC {rc}', flush=True)
    return rc


run_depth80_k1_5()

No pruning/remapping, GPU stream scheduling, finalization, or distributed reconstruction semantics changed; `route_packed.source_rank + parent_idx` remain the only rank/index linkage. Kaggle notebooks now use `HISTORY_MODE=static_hybrid`, `HISTORY_SLOT_COUNT=2`, `HISTORY_RAM_BYTES=28*1000**3`, `HISTORY_DISK_BYTES=49*1000**3`, and `/tmp/beam_history_arena` so sparse history arena files are not packaged as notebook outputs.

CPU appends the suffix after history prefix reconstruction. Local and multi-rank solved-hit selection now compare prefix depth plus suffix length before reconstruction. No K2 descendant expansion was implemented.

from pathlib import Path
import shutil

MOVE_COUNT = 24
HISTORY_ENTRY_BYTES = 16
CANDIDATE_META_BYTES = 32
HISTORY_WRITE_CHUNK_ENTRIES = 1 << 20
KAGGLE_DISK_RESERVE_BYTES = globals().get('KAGGLE_DISK_RESERVE_BYTES', 8 * 1024**3)


def round_up(value: int, alignment: int) -> int:
    return ((value + alignment - 1) // alignment) * alignment


def notebook_cleanup_path(path: Path):
    if not path.exists():
        return
    if path.is_dir():
        shutil.rmtree(path)
    else:
        path.unlink()


def estimate_history_budget_entries(depth_limit: int, k1_radius: int, k2_radius: int, beam_entries: int):
    suffix_radius = k1_radius + k2_radius
    effective_depth = max(depth_limit - suffix_radius, 0)
    frontier_bound = 1
    states_before_target = 0
    for depth in range(effective_depth):
        if frontier_bound < beam_entries:
            frontier_bound = min(beam_entries, frontier_bound * MOVE_COUNT)
        if frontier_bound >= beam_entries:
            full_depths = effective_depth - depth
            return {
                'effective_depth': effective_depth,
                'target_beam_depth': depth,
                'states_before_target_beam': states_before_target,
                'required_entries': states_before_target + full_depths * beam_entries,
            }
        states_before_target += frontier_bound
    return {
        'effective_depth': effective_depth,
        'target_beam_depth': effective_depth,
        'states_before_target_beam': states_before_target,
        'required_entries': states_before_target,
    }


def resolve_local_model_weight_path(model_path_text: str):
    if not model_path_text.startswith('/'):
        return None
    model_path = Path(model_path_text)
    if model_path.is_file():
        return model_path
    if model_path.is_dir():
        matches = sorted(model_path.rglob('*.pth'))
        if matches:
            print(f'preflight_model_weight_file={matches[0]}')
            return matches[0]
    return None


def resolve_model_ref_for_preflight(model_ref: str) -> str:
    model_ref = str(model_ref).strip()
    if model_ref.startswith('kagglehub://'):
        import kagglehub
        handle = model_ref[len('kagglehub://'):]
        resolved = kagglehub.model_download(handle)
        print(f'preflight_kagglehub_model handle={handle} path={resolved}')
        return str(resolved)
    return model_ref


def infer_stream1_output_dim_for_preflight(model_ref: str) -> int:
    model_ref = resolve_model_ref_for_preflight(model_ref)
    weight_path = resolve_local_model_weight_path(model_ref)
    if weight_path is None:
        print(f'preflight_stream1_output_dim=24 source=default_or_unresolved ref={model_ref}')
        return MOVE_COUNT
    try:
        import torch
        checkpoint = torch.load(weight_path, map_location='cpu')
        state_dict = checkpoint
        if isinstance(checkpoint, dict):
            for key in ('state_dict', 'model_state_dict', 'model', 'net', 'module'):
                if isinstance(checkpoint.get(key), dict):
                    state_dict = checkpoint[key]
                    break
        output_dim = int(state_dict['output_layer.weight'].shape[0])
        print(f'preflight_stream1_output_dim={output_dim} source={weight_path}')
        return output_dim
    except Exception as exc:
        print(f'preflight_stream1_output_dim=24 source=failed_infer ref={model_ref} error={type(exc).__name__}:{exc}')
        return MOVE_COUNT


def build_model_test_preflight_table():
    tests = globals().get('STREAM1_MODEL_TESTS') or [{'name': 'default', 'weights': globals().get('STREAM1_MODEL_WEIGHTS_URL', '')}]
    table = {}
    for test in tests:
        name = str(test['name'])
        resolved_ref = resolve_model_ref_for_preflight(str(test.get('weights', '')).strip())
        output_dim = infer_stream1_output_dim_for_preflight(resolved_ref)

from pathlib import Path
import os
import shutil
import subprocess
import urllib.request

WORK_DIR = Path('/kaggle/working')
TMP_DIR = Path('/tmp')
REPO_DIR = TMP_DIR / 'beam_solver'
CUTLASS_DIR = TMP_DIR / 'cutlass'
BUILD_DIR = TMP_DIR / 'beam_build'

def run_checked(cmd, cwd=None, env=None):
    print('+ ' + ' '.join(map(str, cmd)))
    subprocess.run(list(map(str, cmd)), cwd=cwd, env=env, check=True)

SOURCE_ARCHIVE_PATH = Path('/kaggle/input') / SOURCE_ARCHIVE_DATASET / SOURCE_ARCHIVE_NAME

for transient_dir in (REPO_DIR, BUILD_DIR):
    if transient_dir.exists():
        shutil.rmtree(transient_dir)
if SOURCE_ARCHIVE_PATH.exists():
    REPO_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    run_checked(['tar', '-xzf', SOURCE_ARCHIVE_PATH, '-C', REPO_DIR])
else:
    run_checked(['git', 'clone', '--branch', GITHUB_BRANCH, '--depth', '1', GITHUB_REPO_URL, REPO_DIR])

def safe_model_dir_name(name: str) -> str:
    return ''.join(ch if ch.isalnum() or ch in ('-', '_') else '_' for ch in name)


def copy_model_ref_to_source(model_name: str, model_ref: str) -> Path:
    source_path = TMP_DIR / f'stream1_model_weights_{safe_model_dir_name(model_name)}.pth'
    if model_ref.startswith('/'):
        local_weight_path = resolve_local_model_weight_path(model_ref)
        if local_weight_path is None:
            raise FileNotFoundError(f'no .pth file found under model={model_name} ref={model_ref}')
        print(f'stream1_model_weights_source=local model={model_name} path={local_weight_path}')
        shutil.copyfile(local_weight_path, source_path)
    else:
        print(f'stream1_model_weights_source=url model={model_name} url={model_ref}')
        urllib.request.urlretrieve(model_ref, source_path)
    return source_path


def prepare_stream1_weight_dirs():
    weight_dirs = {}
    for model_name, model_info in STREAM1_MODEL_TESTS_PREFLIGHT.items():
        model_ref = str(model_info.get('weights', '')).strip()
        if not model_ref:
            weight_dirs[model_name] = REPO_DIR / 'stream1_weights'
            print(f'stream1_model_weights_source=repo model={model_name} path={weight_dirs[model_name]}')
            continue
        source_path = copy_model_ref_to_source(model_name, model_ref)
        out_dir = TMP_DIR / f'stream1_weights_{safe_model_dir_name(model_name)}'
        if out_dir.exists():
            shutil.rmtree(out_dir)
        run_checked(['python3', REPO_DIR / 'tools' / 'export_stream1_mlp.py', '--weights', source_path, '--out', out_dir], cwd=REPO_DIR)
        weight_dirs[model_name] = out_dir
        print(f'stream1_model_weights_exported model={model_name} out={out_dir}')
    return weight_dirs


STREAM1_WEIGHT_DIRS = prepare_stream1_weight_dirs()


if not (CUTLASS_DIR / 'include').exists():
    if CUTLASS_DIR.exists():
        shutil.rmtree(CUTLASS_DIR)
    run_checked(['git', 'clone', '--depth', '1', 'https://github.com/NVIDIA/cutlass.git', CUTLASS_DIR])

depth_logs = 'ON' if ENABLE_DEPTH_LOGS else 'OFF'
debug_logs = 'ON' if ENABLE_DEBUG_LOGS else 'OFF'
debug_master = 'ON' if ENABLE_DEBUG else 'OFF'
debug_stream_timing = 'ON' if DEBUG_STREAM_TIMING else 'OFF'
debug_inference_trace = 'ON' if DEBUG_INFERENCE_TRACE else 'OFF'

run_checked([

import re
import sys
import threading
import time
import shutil
import subprocess
import pandas as pd

KNOWN_SOLUTION_PATHS = {}

LOG_DIR = WORK_DIR / 'run_logs'
LOG_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
RESULTS_CSV = WORK_DIR / 'beam_run_results.csv'
SUBMISSION_CSV = WORK_DIR / 'submission.csv'
STREAM_BENCH_REPORT = WORK_DIR / 'stream_benchmark.md'
STREAM_BENCH_STDOUT = WORK_DIR / 'stream_benchmark_stdout.log'
SOLVED_RE = re.compile(r'puzzle_solved=(\d+) puzzle_id=(\d+) seconds=([0-9.eE+-]+) solution_length=(-?\d+) solution=(.*)$')


def round_up(value: int, alignment: int) -> int:
    return ((value + alignment - 1) // alignment) * alignment


def cfg_get(cfg: dict, key: str, default):
    return cfg[key] if key in cfg else default


def cfg_model_name(cfg: dict) -> str:
    return str(cfg_get(cfg, 'MODEL_TEST', next(iter(STREAM1_MODEL_TESTS_PREFLIGHT.keys()))))


def cfg_stream1_output_dim(cfg: dict) -> int:
    return int(STREAM1_MODEL_TESTS_PREFLIGHT[cfg_model_name(cfg)]['output_dim'])


def config_derived(cfg: dict) -> dict:
    shard_count = int(cfg_get(cfg, 'SHARD_COUNT', SHARD_COUNT if 'SHARD_COUNT' in globals() else 64))
    alignment = int(cfg_get(cfg, 'STREAM4_BATCH_ALIGNMENT', STREAM4_BATCH_ALIGNMENT))
    scale_ppm = int(cfg_get(cfg, 'SHARD_CAPACITY_SCALE_PPM', SHARD_CAPACITY_SCALE_PPM))
    b_micro = int(cfg_get(cfg, 'B_MICRO', 8192))
    stream3_ring_slots = int(cfg_get(cfg, 'STREAM3_RING_SLOTS', 1))
    output_dim = cfg_stream1_output_dim(cfg)
    rows_per_parent = MOVE_COUNT if output_dim == 1 else 1
    parent_batch = b_micro // rows_per_parent
    stream3_batch = parent_batch * MOVE_COUNT * stream3_ring_slots
    beam_alignment = WORLD_SIZE * shard_count * alignment
    global_beam_effective = round_up(BEAM_WIDTH, beam_alignment)
    local_beam_width = global_beam_effective // WORLD_SIZE
    logical_shard_size = (local_beam_width + shard_count - 1) // shard_count
    shard_capacity_raw = (logical_shard_size * scale_ppm + 999999) // 1000000
    shard_capacity = round_up(shard_capacity_raw, alignment)
    return {
        'b_micro': b_micro,
        'model_test': cfg_model_name(cfg),
        'stream1_output_dim': output_dim,
        'stream3_ring_slots': stream3_ring_slots,
        'stream3_batch_candidates': stream3_batch,
        'shard_count': shard_count,
        'beam_width_alignment': beam_alignment,
        'global_beam_width_effective': global_beam_effective,
        'local_beam_width': local_beam_width,
        'logical_shard_size': logical_shard_size,
        'shard_capacity_candidates': shard_capacity,
    }


def live_log_enabled(rank: int) -> bool:
    if LIVE_LOG_RANKS == 'all':
        return True
    if LIVE_LOG_RANKS == 'none':
        return False
    return rank in set(LIVE_LOG_RANKS)


def stream_rank_log(rank: int, proc, log_path: Path):
    with log_path.open('w', encoding='utf-8') as log:
        assert proc.stdout is not None
        for raw_line in proc.stdout:
            log.write(raw_line)
            log.flush()
            if live_log_enabled(rank):
                print(f'rank={rank} {raw_line}', end='')
                sys.stdout.flush()


def run_stream_benchmark_once():
    if not RUN_STREAM_BENCHMARK:
        return
    env = os.environ.copy()
    env['BEAM_STREAM_BENCH_REPORT'] = str(STREAM_BENCH_REPORT)
    cmd = [str(BUILD_DIR / 'stream_benchmark'), str(START_PUZZLE_ID)]
    print('+ ' + ' '.join(map(str, cmd)))
    proc = subprocess.run(cmd, cwd=REPO_DIR, env=env, stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT, text=True)
    STREAM_BENCH_STDOUT.write_text(proc.stdout, encoding='utf-8')
    print(proc.stdout)
    print(f'stream_benchmark_return_code={proc.returncode}')
    print(f'stream_benchmark_report={STREAM_BENCH_REPORT}')



def cleanup_history_path(path: Path):
    if not path.exists():
        return
    if path.is_dir():
        shutil.rmtree(path)
    else:
        path.unlink()


def run_puzzle(puzzle_id: int, puzzle_index: int, cfg: dict, cfg_index: int):

+    "def choose_history_disk_path():\n",
+    "    roots = []\n",
+    "    for text in [f'/mnt/first/beam_history_{RUN_NAME}', f'/tmp/beam_history_{RUN_NAME}']:\n",
+    "        path = Path(text)\n",
+    "        try:\n",
+    "            if path.exists():\n",
+    "                shutil.rmtree(path)\n",
+    "            path.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n",
+    "            probe = path / '.write_test'\n",
+    "            probe.write_text('ok')\n",
+    "            probe.unlink()\n",
+    "            roots.append((path, shutil.disk_usage(path).free))\n",
+    "        except Exception as exc:\n",
+    "            print(f'history_path_skip={path} reason={type(exc).__name__}:{exc}', flush=True)\n",
+    "    if not roots:\n",
+    "        raise RuntimeError('no writable history disk path found')\n",
+    "    return max(roots, key=lambda item: item[1])\n",
+    "\n",
+    "def torchrun_world_size():\n",
+    "    if int(WORLD_SIZE_OVERRIDE) > 0:\n",
+    "        return int(WORLD_SIZE_OVERRIDE)\n",
+    "    try:\n",
+    "        import torch\n",
+    "        count = torch.cuda.device_count()\n",
+    "    except Exception:\n",
+    "        count = 0\n",
+    "    if count <= 0:\n",
+    "        visible = [x for x in os.environ.get('CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES', '').split(',') if x.strip()]\n",
+    "        count = len(visible)\n",
+    "    if count <= 0:\n",
+    "        raise RuntimeError('could not determine CUDA device count for torchrun')\n",
+    "    return count\n",
+    "\n",
+    "def should_print_live(line: str) -> bool:\n",
+    "    if not LIVE_LOG_FILTER:\n",
+    "        return True\n",
+    "    low = line.lower()\n",
+    "    return (\n",
+    "        'runner_phase=' in line or\n",
+    "        'candidate_history_' in line or\n",
+    "        'history_' in line or\n",
+    "        line.startswith('depth ') or\n",
+    "        'puzzle_' in line or\n",
+    "        'error' in low or\n",
+    "        'exception' in low or\n",
+    "        'RUN_' in line or\n",
+    "        any(line.startswith(prefix) for prefix in LIVE_LOG_RANK_PREFIXES)\n",
+    "    )\n",
+    "\n",
+    "def run_depth80_k1_5_torchrun():\n",
+    "    exe = BUILD_DIR / 'production_runner'\n",
+    "    world_size = torchrun_world_size()\n",
+    "    disk_path, free_bytes = choose_history_disk_path()\n",
+    "    log_path = WORK_DIR / f'{RUN_NAME}.log'\n",
+    "\n",
+    "    env = os.environ.copy()\n",
+    "    env.update(BEAM_ENV)\n",
+    "    env['BEAM_HISTORY_DISK_PATH'] = str(disk_path)\n",
+    "    env.pop('RANK', None)\n",
+    "    env.pop('LOCAL_RANK', None)\n",
+    "    env.pop('WORLD_SIZE', None)\n",
+    "\n",

+    "        '--standalone',\n",

            "source": [
                "import os\n",
                "from pathlib import Path\n",
                "import shutil\n",
                "import subprocess\n",
                "import sys\n",
                "\n",
                "def choose_history_disk_path():\n",
                "    roots = []\n",
                "    for text in [f'/mnt/first/beam_history_{RUN_NAME}', f'/tmp/beam_history_{RUN_NAME}']:\n",
                "        path = Path(text)\n",
                "        try:\n",
                "            if path.exists():\n",
                "                shutil.rmtree(path)\n",
                "            path.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n",
                "            probe = path / '.write_test'\n",
                "            probe.write_text('ok')\n",
                "            probe.unlink()\n",
                "            roots.append((path, shutil.disk_usage(path).free))\n",
                "        except Exception as exc:\n",
                "            print(f'history_path_skip={path} reason={type(exc).__name__}:{exc}', flush=True)\n",
                "    if not roots:\n",
                "        raise RuntimeError('no writable history disk path found')\n",
                "    return max(roots, key=lambda item: item[1])\n",
                "\n",
                "def torchrun_world_size():\n",
                "    if int(WORLD_SIZE_OVERRIDE) > 0:\n",
                "        return int(WORLD_SIZE_OVERRIDE)\n",
                "    try:\n",
                "        import torch\n",
                "        count = torch.cuda.device_count()\n",
                "    except Exception:\n",
                "        count = 0\n",
                "    if count <= 0:\n",
                "        visible = [x for x in os.environ.get('CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES', '').split(',') if x.strip()]\n",
                "        count = len(visible)\n",
                "    if count <= 0:\n",
                "        raise RuntimeError('could not determine CUDA device count for torchrun')\n",
                "    return count\n",
                "\n",
                "def should_print_live(line: str) -> bool:\n",
                "    if not LIVE_LOG_FILTER:\n",
                "        return True\n",
                "    low = line.lower()\n",
                "    return (\n",
                "        'runner_phase=' in line or\n",
                "        'candidate_history_' in line or\n",
                "        'history_' in line or\n",
                "        line.startswith('depth ') or\n",
                "        'puzzle_' in line or\n",
                "        'error' in low or\n",
                "        'exception' in low or\n",
                "        'RUN_' in line or\n",
                "        any(line.startswith(prefix) for prefix in LIVE_LOG_RANK_PREFIXES)\n",
                "    )\n",
                "\n",
                "def run_depth80_k1_5_torchrun():\n",
                "    exe = BUILD_DIR / 'production_runner'\n",
                "    world_size = torchrun_world_size()\n",
                "    disk_path, free_bytes = choose_history_disk_path()\n",
                "    log_path = WORK_DIR / f'{RUN_NAME}.log'\n",
                "\n",
                "    env = os.environ.copy()\n",
                "    env.update(BEAM_ENV)\n",
                "    env['BEAM_HISTORY_DISK_PATH'] = str(disk_path)\n",
                "    env.pop('RANK', None)\n",
                "    env.pop('LOCAL_RANK', None)\n",
                "    env.pop('WORLD_SIZE', None)\n",
                "\n",
                "    cmd = [\n",
                "        sys.executable, '-m', 'torch.distributed.run',\n",
                "        '--standalone',\n",
                "        '--no-python',\n",
                "        '--nnodes=1',\n",
                "        f'--nproc-per-node={world_size}',\n",
                "        str(exe), str(PUZZLE_ID), str(DEPTH_LIMIT), str(BEAM_WIDTH),\n",
                "    ]\n",
                "\n",
                "    print('RUN_DEPTH80_K1_5_H100_TORCHRUN_START', flush=True)\n",
                "    print('cmd=', ' '.join(map(str, cmd)), flush=True)\n",
                "    print('cwd=', REPO_DIR, flush=True)\n",
                "    print('world_size=', world_size, flush=True)\n",
                "    print('global_beam=', BEAM_WIDTH, flush=True)\n",
                "    print('history_disk_path=', disk_path, flush=True)\n",
                "    print('history_disk_free_gib=', round(free_bytes / 1024**3, 2), flush=True)\n",
                "    print('history_ram_gib=96 history_disk_gib=192 k1=5 k2=0', flush=True)\n",
                "    print('log_path=', log_path, flush=True)\n",
                "\n",
                "    with log_path.open('w', buffering=1, encoding='utf-8') as log:\n",
                "        proc = subprocess.Popen(cmd, cwd=REPO_DIR, env=env, stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT, text=True, bufsize=1)\n",
                "        assert proc.stdout is not None\n",
                "        for line in proc.stdout:\n",
                "            log.write(line)\n",
                "            if should_print_live(line):\n",
                "                print(line, end='', flush=True)\n",
                "        rc = proc.wait()\n",
                "    print(f'RUN_DEPTH80_K1_5_H100_TORCHRUN_RC {rc}', flush=True)\n",
                "    return rc\n",
                "\n",
                "rc = run_depth80_k1_5_torchrun()\n",
                "if rc != 0:\n",
                "    raise SystemExit(rc)\n"
            ]
        }
    ],

            "outputs": [],
            "source": [
                "import os\n",
                "from pathlib import Path\n",
                "import shutil\n",
                "import subprocess\n",
                "import sys\n",
                "\n",
                "def choose_history_disk_path():\n",
                "    roots = []\n",
                "    for text in [f'/mnt/first/beam_history_{RUN_NAME}', f'/tmp/beam_history_{RUN_NAME}']:\n",
                "        path = Path(text)\n",
                "        try:\n",
                "            if path.exists():\n",
                "                shutil.rmtree(path)\n",
                "            path.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n",
                "            probe = path / '.write_test'\n",
                "            probe.write_text('ok')\n",
                "            probe.unlink()\n",
                "            roots.append((path, shutil.disk_usage(path).free))\n",
                "        except Exception as exc:\n",
                "            print(f'history_path_skip={path} reason={type(exc).__name__}:{exc}', flush=True)\n",
                "    if not roots:\n",
                "        raise RuntimeError('no writable history disk path found')\n",
                "    return max(roots, key=lambda item: item[1])\n",
                "\n",
                "def torchrun_world_size():\n",
                "    nnodes = int(TORCHRUN_NNODES)\n",
                "    nproc_per_node = int(TORCHRUN_NPROC_PER_NODE)\n",
                "    if nnodes <= 0 or nproc_per_node <= 0:\n",
                "        raise ValueError(f'invalid torchrun shape: nnodes={nnodes} nproc_per_node={nproc_per_node}')\n",
                "    return nnodes * nproc_per_node\n",
                "\n",
                "def should_print_live(line: str) -> bool:\n",
                "    if not LIVE_LOG_FILTER:\n",
                "        return True\n",
                "    low = line.lower()\n",
                "    return (\n",
                "        'runner_phase=' in line or\n",
                "        'candidate_history_' in line or\n",
                "        'history_' in line or\n",
                "        line.startswith('depth ') or\n",
                "        'puzzle_' in line or\n",
                "        'error' in low or\n",
                "        'exception' in low or\n",
                "        'RUN_' in line or\n",
                "        any(line.startswith(prefix) for prefix in LIVE_LOG_RANK_PREFIXES)\n",
                "    )\n",
                "\n",
                "def run_depth80_k1_5_torchrun():\n",
                "    exe = BUILD_DIR / 'production_runner'\n",
                "    world_size = torchrun_world_size()\n",
                "    disk_path, free_bytes = choose_history_disk_path()\n",
                "    log_path = WORK_DIR / f'{RUN_NAME}.log'\n",
                "\n",
                "    env = os.environ.copy()\n",
                "    env.update(BEAM_ENV)\n",
                "    env['BEAM_HISTORY_DISK_PATH'] = str(disk_path)\n",
                "    env.pop('RANK', None)\n",
                "    env.pop('LOCAL_RANK', None)\n",
                "    env.pop('WORLD_SIZE', None)\n",
                "\n",
                "    cmd = [\n",
                "        sys.executable, '-m', 'torch.distributed.run',\n",
                "        '--standalone',\n",
                "        '--no-python',\n",
                "        f'--nnodes={int(TORCHRUN_NNODES)}',\n",
                "        f'--nproc-per-node={int(TORCHRUN_NPROC_PER_NODE)}',\n",
                "        str(exe), str(PUZZLE_ID), str(DEPTH_LIMIT), str(BEAM_WIDTH),\n",
                "    ]\n",
                "\n",
                "    print('RUN_DEPTH80_K1_5_H100_TORCHRUN_START', flush=True)\n",
                "    print('cmd=', ' '.join(map(str, cmd)), flush=True)\n",
                "    print('cwd=', REPO_DIR, flush=True)\n",
                "    print('torchrun_nnodes=', int(TORCHRUN_NNODES), flush=True)\n",
                "    print('torchrun_nproc_per_node=', int(TORCHRUN_NPROC_PER_NODE), flush=True)\n",
                "    print('world_size_from_torchrun_shape=', world_size, flush=True)\n",
                "    print('global_beam=', BEAM_WIDTH, flush=True)\n",
                "    print('history_disk_path=', disk_path, flush=True)\n",
                "    print('history_disk_free_gib=', round(free_bytes / 1024**3, 2), flush=True)\n",
                "    print('history_ram_gib=96 history_disk_gib=192 k1=5 k2=0', flush=True)\n",
                "    print('log_path=', log_path, flush=True)\n",
                "\n",
                "    with log_path.open('w', buffering=1, encoding='utf-8') as log:\n",
                "        proc = subprocess.Popen(cmd, cwd=REPO_DIR, env=env, stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT, text=True, bufsize=1)\n",
                "        assert proc.stdout is not None\n",
                "        for line in proc.stdout:\n",
                "            log.write(line)\n",
                "            if should_print_live(line):\n",
                "                print(line, end='', flush=True)\n",
                "        rc = proc.wait()\n",
                "    print(f'RUN_DEPTH80_K1_5_H100_TORCHRUN_RC {rc}', flush=True)\n",
                "    return rc\n",
                "\n",
                "rc = run_depth80_k1_5_torchrun()\n",
                "if rc != 0:\n",
                "    raise SystemExit(rc)\n"
            ]
        }
    ],

            "pygments_lexer": "ipython3"
        }
    },
    "nbformat": 4,
    "nbformat_minor": 5
}

## 61. Codex

Time: `2026-06-03T19:53:59.622Z` · source line `103`

````text
Да, по запуску ранков - верно: ноут теперь задает topology через `TORCHRUN_NNODES` и `TORCHRUN_NPROC_PER_NODE`, а `production_runner` получает от torchrun честные `WORLD_SIZE`, `RANK`, `LOCAL_RANK`.

Для 8 карт на одной ноде:

```python
TORCHRUN_NNODES = 1
TORCHRUN_NPROC_PER_NODE = 8
```

Для 100 карт, если это 10 нод по 10 GPU:

```python
TORCHRUN_NNODES = 10
TORCHRUN_NPROC_PER_NODE = 10
```

Но есть практическая оговорка: текущий ноутбук использует `--standalone`, это подходит для одной ноды. Для реального multi-node на 100 карт нужно будет заменить запуск на rendezvous-параметры torchrun: `--rdzv_backend`, `--rdzv_endpoint`, `--node_rank` и одинаковые `--nnodes/--nproc-per-node` на всех нодах. Сам контракт ранков для C++ runner от этого останется тем же.

То есть: для 8 GPU single-node уже да. Для 100 GPU multi-node логика правильная, но ноуту нужен небольшой multi-node launcher-блок вместо `--standalone`.
````

Но есть практическая оговорка: текущий ноутбук использует `--standalone`, это подходит для одной ноды. Для реального multi-node на 100 карт нужно будет заменить запуск на rendezvous-параметры torchrun: `--rdzv_backend`, `--rdzv_endpoint`, `--node_rank` и одинаковые `--nnodes/--nproc-per-node` на всех нодах. Сам контракт ранков для C++ runner от этого останется тем же.

То есть: для 8 GPU single-node уже да. Для 100 GPU multi-node логика правильная, но ноуту нужен небольшой multi-node launcher-блок вместо `--standalone`.
````

                "import os\n",
                "from pathlib import Path\n",
                "import shutil\n",
                "import subprocess\n",
                "import sys\n",
                "\n",
                "def choose_history_disk_path():\n",
                "    roots = []\n",
                "    for text in [f'/mnt/first/beam_history_{RUN_NAME}', f'/tmp/beam_history_{RUN_NAME}']:\n",
                "        path = Path(text)\n",
                "        try:\n",
                "            if path.exists():\n",
                "                shutil.rmtree(path)\n",
                "            path.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n",
                "            probe = path / '.write_test'\n",
                "            probe.write_text('ok')\n",
                "            probe.unlink()\n",
                "            roots.append((path, shutil.disk_usage(path).free))\n",
                "        except Exception as exc:\n",
                "            print(f'history_path_skip={path} reason={type(exc).__name__}:{exc}', flush=True)\n",
                "    if not roots:\n",
                "        raise RuntimeError('no writable history disk path found')\n",
                "    return max(roots, key=lambda item: item[1])\n",
                "\n",
                "def torchrun_world_size():\n",
                "    nnodes = int(TORCHRUN_NNODES)\n",
                "    nproc_per_node = int(TORCHRUN_NPROC_PER_NODE)\n",
                "    if nnodes <= 0 or nproc_per_node <= 0:\n",
                "        raise ValueError(f'invalid torchrun shape: nnodes={nnodes} nproc_per_node={nproc_per_node}')\n",
                "    return nnodes * nproc_per_node\n",
                "\n",
                "def should_print_live(line: str) -> bool:\n",
                "    if not LIVE_LOG_FILTER:\n",
                "        return True\n",
                "    low = line.lower()\n",
                "    return (\n",
                "        'runner_phase=' in line or\n",
                "        'candidate_history_' in line or\n",
                "        'history_' in line or\n",
                "        line.startswith('depth ') or\n",
                "        'puzzle_' in line or\n",
                "        'error' in low or\n",
                "        'exception' in low or\n",
                "        'RUN_' in line or\n",
                "        any(line.startswith(prefix) for prefix in LIVE_LOG_RANK_PREFIXES)\n",
                "    )\n",
                "\n",
                "def run_depth80_k1_5_torchrun():\n",
                "    exe = BUILD_DIR / 'production_runner'\n",
                "    world_size = torchrun_world_size()\n",
                "    disk_path, free_bytes = choose_history_disk_path()\n",
                "    log_path = WORK_DIR / f'{RUN_NAME}.log'\n",
                "\n",
                "    env = os.environ.copy()\n",
                "    env.update(BEAM_ENV)\n",
                "    env['BEAM_HISTORY_DISK_PATH'] = str(disk_path)\n",
                "    env.pop('RANK', None)\n",
                "    env.pop('LOCAL_RANK', None)\n",
                "    env.pop('WORLD_SIZE', None)\n",
                "\n",
                "    cmd = [\n",
                "        sys.executable, '-m', 'torch.distributed.run',\n",
                "        '--no-python',\n",
                "        f'--nnodes={int(TORCHRUN_NNODES)}',\n",
                "        f'--nproc-per-node={int(TORCHRUN_NPROC_PER_NODE)}',\n",
                "    ]\n",
                "    if TORCHRUN_MODE == 'single_node':\n",
                "        cmd.append('--standalone')\n",
                "    elif TORCHRUN_MODE == 'multi_node':\n",
                "        cmd.extend([\n",
                "            f'--node-rank={int(TORCHRUN_NODE_RANK)}',\n",
                "            f'--rdzv-backend={TORCHRUN_RDZV_BACKEND}',\n",
                "            f'--rdzv-endpoint={TORCHRUN_RDZV_ENDPOINT}',\n",
                "            f'--rdzv-id={TORCHRUN_RDZV_ID}',\n",
                "        ])\n",
                "    else:\n",
                "        raise ValueError(f'unknown TORCHRUN_MODE={TORCHRUN_MODE!r}')\n",
                "    cmd.extend([str(exe), str(PUZZLE_ID), str(DEPTH_LIMIT), str(BEAM_WIDTH)])\n",
                "\n",
                "    print('RUN_DEPTH80_K1_5_H100_TORCHRUN_START', flush=True)\n",
                "    print('cmd=', ' '.join(map(str, cmd)), flush=True)\n",
                "    print('cwd=', REPO_DIR, flush=True)\n",
                "    print('torchrun_mode=', TORCHRUN_MODE, flush=True)\n",
                "    print('torchrun_nnodes=', int(TORCHRUN_NNODES), flush=True)\n",
                "    print('torchrun_nproc_per_node=', int(TORCHRUN_NPROC_PER_NODE), flush=True)\n",
                "    print('torchrun_node_rank=', int(TORCHRUN_NODE_RANK), flush=True)\n",
                "    if TORCHRUN_MODE == 'multi_node':\n",
                "        print('torchrun_rdzv_backend=', TORCHRUN_RDZV_BACKEND, flush=True)\n",
                "        print('torchrun_rdzv_endpoint=', TORCHRUN_RDZV_ENDPOINT, flush=True)\n",
                "        print('torchrun_rdzv_id=', TORCHRUN_RDZV_ID, flush=True)\n",
                "    print('world_size_from_torchrun_shape=', world_size, flush=True)\n",
                "    print('global_beam=', BEAM_WIDTH, flush=True)\n",
                "    print('history_disk_path=', disk_path, flush=True)\n",
                "    print('history_disk_free_gib=', round(free_bytes / 1024**3, 2), flush=True)\n",
                "    print('history_ram_gib=96 history_disk_gib=192 k1=5 k2=0', flush=True)\n",
                "    print('log_path=', log_path, flush=True)\n",
                "\n",
                "    with log_path.open('w', buffering=1, encoding='utf-8') as log:\n",
                "        proc = subprocess.Popen(cmd, cwd=REPO_DIR, env=env, stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT, text=True, bufsize=1)\n",
                "        assert proc.stdout is not None\n",

                "        rc = proc.wait()\n",
                "    print(f'RUN_DEPTH80_K1_5_H100_TORCHRUN_RC {rc}', flush=True)\n",
                "    return rc\n",
                "\n",
                "rc = run_depth80_k1_5_torchrun()\n",
                "if rc != 0:\n",
                "    raise SystemExit(rc)\n"
            ]

                "import os\n",
                "from pathlib import Path\n",
                "import shutil\n",
                "import subprocess\n",
                "import sys\n",
                "\n",
                "def choose_history_disk_path():\n",
                "    roots = []\n",
                "    for text in [f'/mnt/first/beam_history_{RUN_NAME}', f'/tmp/beam_history_{RUN_NAME}']:\n",
                "        path = Path(text)\n",
                "        try:\n",
                "            if path.exists():\n",
                "                shutil.rmtree(path)\n",
                "            path.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n",
                "            probe = path / '.write_test'\n",
                "            probe.write_text('ok')\n",
                "            probe.unlink()\n",
                "            roots.append((path, shutil.disk_usage(path).free))\n",
                "        except Exception as exc:\n",
                "            print(f'history_path_skip={path} reason={type(exc).__name__}:{exc}', flush=True)\n",
                "    if not roots:\n",
                "        raise RuntimeError('no writable history disk path found')\n",
                "    return max(roots, key=lambda item: item[1])\n",
                "\n",
                "def torchrun_world_size():\n",
                "    nnodes = int(TORCHRUN_NNODES)\n",
                "    nproc_per_node = int(TORCHRUN_NPROC_PER_NODE)\n",
                "    if nnodes <= 0 or nproc_per_node <= 0:\n",
                "        raise ValueError(f'invalid torchrun shape: nnodes={nnodes} nproc_per_node={nproc_per_node}')\n",
                "    return nnodes * nproc_per_node\n",
                "\n",
                "def should_print_live(line: str) -> bool:\n",
                "    if not LIVE_LOG_FILTER:\n",
                "        return True\n",
                "    low = line.lower()\n",
                "    return (\n",
                "        'runner_phase=' in line or\n",
                "        'candidate_history_' in line or\n",
                "        'history_' in line or\n",
                "        line.startswith('depth ') or\n",
                "        'puzzle_' in line or\n",
                "        'error' in low or\n",
                "        'exception' in low or\n",
                "        'RUN_' in line or\n",
                "        any(line.startswith(prefix) for prefix in LIVE_LOG_RANK_PREFIXES)\n",
                "    )\n",
                "\n",
                "def run_depth80_k1_5_torchrun():\n",
                "    exe = BUILD_DIR / 'production_runner'\n",
                "    world_size = torchrun_world_size()\n",
                "    disk_path, free_bytes = choose_history_disk_path()\n",
                "    log_path = WORK_DIR / f'{RUN_NAME}.log'\n",
                "\n",
                "    env = os.environ.copy()\n",
                "    env.update(BEAM_ENV)\n",
                "    env['BEAM_HISTORY_DISK_PATH'] = str(disk_path)\n",
                "    env.pop('RANK', None)\n",
                "    env.pop('LOCAL_RANK', None)\n",
                "    env.pop('WORLD_SIZE', None)\n",
                "\n",
                "    cmd = [\n",
                "        sys.executable, '-m', 'torch.distributed.run',\n",
                "        '--no-python',\n",
                "        f'--nnodes={int(TORCHRUN_NNODES)}',\n",
                "        f'--nproc-per-node={int(TORCHRUN_NPROC_PER_NODE)}',\n",
                "    ]\n",
                "    if TORCHRUN_MODE == 'single_node':\n",
                "        cmd.append('--standalone')\n",
                "    elif TORCHRUN_MODE == 'multi_node':\n",
                "        cmd.extend([\n",
                "            f'--node-rank={int(TORCHRUN_NODE_RANK)}',\n",
                "            f'--rdzv-backend={TORCHRUN_RDZV_BACKEND}',\n",
                "            f'--rdzv-endpoint={TORCHRUN_RDZV_ENDPOINT}',\n",
                "            f'--rdzv-id={TORCHRUN_RDZV_ID}',\n",
                "        ])\n",
                "    else:\n",
                "        raise ValueError(f'unknown TORCHRUN_MODE={TORCHRUN_MODE!r}')\n",
                "    cmd.extend([str(exe), str(PUZZLE_ID), str(DEPTH_LIMIT), str(BEAM_WIDTH)])\n",
                "\n",
                "    print('RUN_DEPTH80_K1_5_H100_TORCHRUN_START', flush=True)\n",
                "    print('cmd=', ' '.join(map(str, cmd)), flush=True)\n",
                "    print('cwd=', REPO_DIR, flush=True)\n",
                "    print('torchrun_mode=', TORCHRUN_MODE, flush=True)\n",
                "    print('torchrun_nnodes=', int(TORCHRUN_NNODES), flush=True)\n",
                "    print('torchrun_nproc_per_node=', int(TORCHRUN_NPROC_PER_NODE), flush=True)\n",
                "    print('torchrun_node_rank=', int(TORCHRUN_NODE_RANK), flush=True)\n",
                "    if TORCHRUN_MODE == 'multi_node':\n",
                "        print('torchrun_rdzv_backend=', TORCHRUN_RDZV_BACKEND, flush=True)\n",
                "        print('torchrun_rdzv_endpoint=', TORCHRUN_RDZV_ENDPOINT, flush=True)\n",
                "        print('torchrun_rdzv_id=', TORCHRUN_RDZV_ID, flush=True)\n",
                "    print('world_size_from_torchrun_shape=', world_size, flush=True)\n",
                "    print('global_beam=', BEAM_WIDTH, flush=True)\n",
                "    print('history_disk_path=', disk_path, flush=True)\n",
                "    print('history_disk_free_gib=', round(free_bytes / 1024**3, 2), flush=True)\n",
                "    print('history_ram_gib=96 history_disk_gib=192 k1=5 k2=0', flush=True)\n",
                "    print('log_path=', log_path, flush=True)\n",

# Project Agent Rules

## Operating Rule
- Read `AGENTS.md` before project edits.
- Update `memory/CHANGELOG.md` after meaningful code, config, or architecture changes.
- Update `memory/PROMPTS.md` with user prompts that define requirements.
- Store test outputs, logs, and verification notes under `test_results/`.
- Prefer agent-centered code: explicit contracts, deterministic tests, small files, low hidden state.

## Architecture Source
- Primary architecture contract: `ARCHITECTURE_NEED.md`.
- Current Stream 4 implementation rule: use threshold + compact + CUB/fixed-temp sort/reduce + compact; do not use Stream 4 hash-table dedup.

## Implementation Guardrails
- `State128` logical bytes are `v[0..119]`.
- `State128` padding bytes are `v[120..127]`.
- Persistent frontier padding must be zero.
- `FinalResponse` may temporarily store `target_local_idx` in `v[120..123]`.
- `Hash128` is one logical 128-bit key stored as `{lo, hi}`.
- `CandidateMeta` must remain 32 bytes and 32-byte aligned.
- Stream 3 payload id must be original candidate id, not compact index.
- Stream 4 must not apply shard top-k or semantic shard cap.

## 140. Codex

Time: `2026-06-03T21:57:01.376Z` · source line `239`

```text
По видимому логу C++ упал почти сразу после torchrun, но `what(): ...` не попал в Kaggle output из-за фильтра печати. Самый вероятный конкретный косяк уже виден: notebook был отправлен на 2xT4, а сборка жестко стоит `-DBEAM_CUDA_ARCHITECTURES=90`, то есть бинарник собран только под H100, не под T4 `sm_75`. Я поправлю ноут: сделаю `CUDA_ARCHITECTURES` конфигом и для Kaggle 2xT4 поставлю `75`, плюс расширю live-фильтр, чтобы такие `terminate/what()` больше не прятались.
```

+    "\n",
+    "def run_checked(cmd, cwd=None, env=None):\n",

+    "def safe_name(text):\n",

+    "\n",
+    "def resolve_weight_path(ref):\n",

+    "    if path.is_file():\n",
+    "        return path\n",
+    "    if path.is_dir():\n",
+    "        matches = sorted(path.rglob('*.pth'))\n",
+    "        if matches:\n",
+    "            return matches[0]\n",
+    "    raise FileNotFoundError(f'no .pth file found for {ref}')\n",
+    "\n",

+    "    if path.exists():\n",
+    "        shutil.rmtree(path)\n",

+    "\n",

+    "    ref = str(model.get('weights', '')).strip()\n",
+    "    if not ref:\n",
+    "        STREAM1_WEIGHT_DIRS[name] = REPO_DIR / 'stream1_weights'\n",
+    "        print(f'stream1_model_weights_source=repo model={name} path={STREAM1_WEIGHT_DIRS[name]}')\n",
+    "        continue\n",
+    "    source_path = TMP_DIR / f'stream1_model_{safe_name(name)}.pth'\n",
+    "    if ref.startswith('/'):\n",
+    "        shutil.copyfile(resolve_weight_path(ref), source_path)\n",
+    "    else:\n",
+    "        urllib.request.urlretrieve(ref, source_path)\n",
+    "    out_dir = TMP_DIR / f'stream1_weights_{safe_name(name)}'\n",
+    "    if out_dir.exists():\n",
+    "        shutil.rmtree(out_dir)\n",
+    "    run_checked(['python3', REPO_DIR / 'tools' / 'export_stream1_mlp.py', '--weights', source_path, '--out', out_dir], cwd=REPO_DIR)\n",
+    "    STREAM1_WEIGHT_DIRS[name] = out_dir\n",

+   "cell_type": "code",

+   "source": [
+    "import os\n",
+    "from pathlib import Path\n",
+    "import shutil\n",
+    "import subprocess\n",
+    "import sys\n",
+    "import time\n",
+    "\n",
+    "def torchrun_world_size():\n",
+    "    nnodes = int(TORCHRUN_NNODES)\n",
+    "    nproc_per_node = int(TORCHRUN_NPROC_PER_NODE)\n",
+    "    if nnodes <= 0 or nproc_per_node <= 0:\n",
+    "        raise ValueError(f'invalid torchrun shape: nnodes={nnodes} nproc_per_node={nproc_per_node}')\n",
+    "    return nnodes * nproc_per_node\n",
+    "\n",
+    "def cleanup_path(path):\n",
+    "    path = Path(path)\n",
+    "    if path.exists():\n",
+    "        if path.is_dir():\n",
+    "            shutil.rmtree(path)\n",
+    "        else:\n",
+    "            path.unlink()\n",
+    "\n",
+    "def should_print_live(line):\n",
+    "    low = line.lower()\n",
+    "    return (\n",
+    "        'runner_phase=' in line or\n",
+    "        'candidate_history_' in line or\n",
+    "        'history_' in line or\n",
+    "        line.startswith('depth ') or\n",
+    "        'puzzle_' in line or\n",
+    "        'error' in low or\n",
+    "        'exception' in low or\n",
+    "        'terminate called' in low or\n",
+    "        'what():' in low or\n",
+    "        'childfailed' in low or\n",
+    "        'traceback' in low or\n",
+    "        'RUN_' in line\n",
+    "    )\n",
+    "\n",
+    "def cfg_get(cfg, key, default):\n",
+    "    return cfg[key] if key in cfg else default\n",
+    "\n",
+    "def env_for_config(cfg, puzzle_id):\n",
+    "    model_name = cfg_get(cfg, 'MODEL_TEST', STREAM1_MODEL_TESTS[0]['name'])\n",
+    "    weight_dir = STREAM1_WEIGHT_DIRS[model_name]\n",
+    "    target_weights = REPO_DIR / 'stream1_weights'\n",
+    "    if target_weights.exists() and target_weights.resolve() != Path(weight_dir).resolve():\n",
+    "        shutil.rmtree(target_weights)\n",
+    "    if not target_weights.exists():\n",
+    "        shutil.copytree(weight_dir, target_weights)\n",
+    "\n",
+    "    history_path = Path(HISTORY_DISK_PATH) / f'torchrun_p{puzzle_id}_{safe_name(cfg_get(cfg, \"name\", \"config\"))}'\n",
+    "    cleanup_path(history_path)\n",
+    "    history_path.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n",
+    "\n",
+    "    env = os.environ.copy()\n",
+    "    env.update({\n",
+    "        'BEAM_RUNTIME_CONFIG_MODE': RUNTIME_CONFIG_MODE,\n",
+    "        'BEAM_SHARD_COUNT': str(cfg_get(cfg, 'SHARD_COUNT', SHARD_COUNT)),\n",
+    "        'BEAM_SHARD_CAPACITY_SCALE_PPM': str(SHARD_CAPACITY_SCALE_PPM),\n",
+    "        'BEAM_SHARD_BUFFER_COUNT': str(SHARD_BUFFER_COUNT),\n",
+    "        'BEAM_GLOBAL_SPILL_CAPACITY': str(GLOBAL_SPILL_CAPACITY),\n",
+    "        'BEAM_STREAM5_RECV_CAPACITY_SCALE_PPM': str(STREAM5_RECV_CAPACITY_SCALE_PPM),\n",
+    "        'BEAM_GPU_HEADROOM_BYTES': str(GPU_HEADROOM_BYTES),\n",
+    "        'BEAM_B_MICRO': str(cfg_get(cfg, 'B_MICRO', B_MICRO)),\n",
+    "        'BEAM_STREAM1_CONCURRENCY': str(cfg_get(cfg, 'STREAM1_CONCURRENCY', STREAM1_CONCURRENCY)),\n",
+    "        'BEAM_STREAM3_RING_SLOTS': str(cfg_get(cfg, 'STREAM3_RING_SLOTS', STREAM3_RING_SLOTS)),\n",
+    "        'BEAM_STREAM4_BATCH_CANDIDATES': str(cfg_get(cfg, 'STREAM4_BATCH_CANDIDATES', STREAM4_BATCH_CANDIDATES)),\n",
+    "        'BEAM_STREAM4_TRIGGER_CANDIDATES': str(cfg_get(cfg, 'STREAM4_TRIGGER_CANDIDATES', STREAM4_TRIGGER_CANDIDATES)),\n",
+    "        'BEAM_STREAM4_ACTIVE_SORT_SLOTS': str(cfg_get(cfg, 'STREAM4_ACTIVE_SORT_SLOTS', STREAM4_ACTIVE_SORT_SLOTS)),\n",
+    "        'BEAM_HISTORY_MODE': str(cfg_get(cfg, 'HISTORY_MODE', HISTORY_MODE)),\n",
+    "        'BEAM_HISTORY_RAM_BYTES': str(HISTORY_RAM_BYTES),\n",
+    "        'BEAM_HISTORY_DISK_BYTES': str(HISTORY_DISK_BYTES),\n",
+    "        'BEAM_HISTORY_DISK_PATH': str(history_path),\n",
+    "        'BEAM_HISTORY_SLOT_COUNT': str(HISTORY_SLOT_COUNT),\n",
+    "        'BEAM_HISTORY_WORKERS': str(HISTORY_WORKERS),\n",
+    "        'BEAM_SOLVED_NEIGHBORHOOD_RADIUS': str(SOLVED_NEIGHBORHOOD_RADIUS),\n",
+    "        'BEAM_SOLVED_NEIGHBORHOOD_MAX_ENTRIES': str(SOLVED_NEIGHBORHOOD_MAX_ENTRIES),\n",
+    "        'BEAM_STREAM2_SUFFIX_RADIUS': str(STREAM2_SUFFIX_RADIUS),\n",
+    "        'BEAM_STREAM2_SUFFIX_BACKEND': str(STREAM2_SUFFIX_BACKEND),\n",
+    "        'BEAM_STREAM2_SUFFIX_MAX_COUNT': str(STREAM2_SUFFIX_MAX_COUNT),\n",
+    "        'BEAM_DEPTH_LOG_EVERY': str(DEPTH_LOG_EVERY),\n",
+    "    })\n",
+    "    env.pop('WORLD_SIZE', None)\n",
+    "    env.pop('RANK', None)\n",
+    "    env.pop('LOCAL_RANK', None)\n",
+    "    return env, history_path\n",
+    "\n",
+    "def run_one(puzzle_id, cfg):\n",
+    "    world_size = torchrun_world_size()\n",
+    "    cfg_name = cfg_get(cfg, 'name', 'config')\n",
+    "    env, history_path = env_for_config(cfg, puzzle_id)\n",
+    "    log_path = WORK_DIR / f'torchrun_{safe_name(cfg_name)}_p{puzzle_id}.log'\n",
+    "    cmd = [\n",
+    "        sys.executable, '-m', 'torch.distributed.run',\n",
+    "        '--no-python',\n",
+    "        f'--nnodes={int(TORCHRUN_NNODES)}',\n",
+    "        f'--nproc-per-node={int(TORCHRUN_NPROC_PER_NODE)}',\n",
+    "        f'--node-rank={int(TORCHRUN_NODE_RANK)}',\n",
+    "        f'--rdzv-backend={TORCHRUN_RDZV_BACKEND}',\n",
+    "        f'--rdzv-endpoint={TORCHRUN_RDZV_ENDPOINT}',\n",
+    "        f'--rdzv-id={TORCHRUN_RDZV_ID}',\n",
+    "        str(BUILD_DIR / 'production_runner'), str(puzzle_id), str(DEPTH_LIMIT), str(BEAM_WIDTH),\n",
+    "    ]\n",
+    "    print('RUN_T4_TORCHRUN_START', flush=True)\n",
+    "    print('cmd=', ' '.join(map(str, cmd)), flush=True)\n",
+    "    print('config=', cfg_name, flush=True)\n",
+    "    print('world_size_from_torchrun_shape=', world_size, flush=True)\n",
+    "    print('global_beam=', BEAM_WIDTH, flush=True)\n",
+    "    print('history_path=', history_path, flush=True)\n",
+    "    print('log_path=', log_path, flush=True)\n",
+    "    start = time.time()\n",
+    "    with log_path.open('w', buffering=1, encoding='utf-8') as log:\n",
+    "        proc = subprocess.Popen(cmd, cwd=REPO_DIR, env=env, stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT, text=True, bufsize=1)\n",
+    "        assert proc.stdout is not None\n",
+    "        for line in proc.stdout:\n",
+    "            log.write(line)\n",
+    "            if should_print_live(line):\n",
+    "                print(line, end='', flush=True)\n",
+    "        rc = proc.wait()\n",
+    "    elapsed = time.time() - start\n",
+    "    print(f'RUN_T4_TORCHRUN_RC {rc} seconds={elapsed:.3f}', flush=True)\n",
+    "    cleanup_path(history_path)\n",
+    "    return rc\n",
+    "\n",
+    "results = []\n",
+    "for cfg in SWEEP_CONFIGS:\n",
+    "    for offset in range(PUZZLE_COUNT):\n",
+    "        puzzle_id = START_PUZZLE_ID + offset\n",
+    "        rc = run_one(puzzle_id, cfg)\n",
+    "        results.append({'config': cfg_get(cfg, 'name', 'config'), 'puzzle_id': puzzle_id, 'return_code': rc})\n",
+    "        if rc != 0 and STOP_ON_FAILURE:\n",
+    "            raise SystemExit(rc)\n",
+    "        if rc != 0:\n",
+    "            raise SystemExit(rc)\n",
+    "results\n"
+   ]
+  }
+ ],
+ "metadata": {
+  "kernelspec": {
+   "display_name": "Python 3",
+   "language": "python",
+   "name": "python3"
+  },
+  "language_info": {
+   "name": "python",
+   "pygments_lexer": "ipython3"
+  }
+ },
+ "nbformat": 4,
+ "nbformat_minor": 5
+}
*** End Patch

```

## 186. Codex

Time: `2026-06-03T22:02:41.084Z` · source line `318`

```text
Проверка JSON/AST прошла. Я намеренно поставил `RUNTIME_CONFIG_MODE="manual"` в новом T4 notebook: в C++ рантайме ручной режим включается именно `BEAM_RUNTIME_CONFIG_MODE=manual`; значение `static_hybrid` относится к `HISTORY_MODE`, а не к runtime config mode.
```

from pathlib import Path
import os
import shutil
import subprocess
import urllib.request

WORK_DIR = Path('/kaggle/working')
TMP_DIR = Path('/tmp')
REPO_DIR = TMP_DIR / 'beam_solver_t4_torchrun'
CUTLASS_DIR = TMP_DIR / 'cutlass'
BUILD_DIR = TMP_DIR / 'beam_build_t4_torchrun'

def run_checked(cmd, cwd=None, env=None):
    print('+ ' + ' '.join(map(str, cmd)), flush=True)
    subprocess.run(list(map(str, cmd)), cwd=cwd, env=env, check=True)

def safe_name(text):
    return ''.join(ch if ch.isalnum() or ch in ('-', '_') else '_' for ch in str(text))

def resolve_weight_path(ref):
    path = Path(ref)
    if path.is_file():
        return path
    if path.is_dir():
        matches = sorted(path.rglob('*.pth'))
        if matches:
            return matches[0]
    raise FileNotFoundError(f'no .pth file found for {ref}')

for path in (REPO_DIR, BUILD_DIR):
    if path.exists():
        shutil.rmtree(path)

run_checked(['git', 'clone', '--branch', GITHUB_BRANCH, '--depth', '1', GITHUB_REPO_URL, REPO_DIR])

STREAM1_WEIGHT_DIRS = {}
for model in STREAM1_MODEL_TESTS:
    name = model['name']
    ref = str(model.get('weights', '')).strip()
    if not ref:
        STREAM1_WEIGHT_DIRS[name] = REPO_DIR / 'stream1_weights'
        print(f'stream1_model_weights_source=repo model={name} path={STREAM1_WEIGHT_DIRS[name]}')
        continue
    source_path = TMP_DIR / f'stream1_model_{safe_name(name)}.pth'
    if ref.startswith('/'):
        shutil.copyfile(resolve_weight_path(ref), source_path)
    else:
        urllib.request.urlretrieve(ref, source_path)
    out_dir = TMP_DIR / f'stream1_weights_{safe_name(name)}'
    if out_dir.exists():
        shutil.rmtree(out_dir)
    run_checked(['python3', REPO_DIR / 'tools' / 'export_stream1_mlp.py', '--weights', source_path, '--out', out_dir], cwd=REPO_DIR)
    STREAM1_WEIGHT_DIRS[name] = out_dir
    print(f'stream1_model_weights_exported model={name} out={out_dir}')

if not (CUTLASS_DIR / 'include').exists():
    if CUTLASS_DIR.exists():
        shutil.rmtree(CUTLASS_DIR)
    run_checked(['git', 'clone', '--depth', '1', 'https://github.com/NVIDIA/cutlass.git', CUTLASS_DIR])

run_checked([
    'cmake', '-S', REPO_DIR, '-B', BUILD_DIR, '-GNinja',
    '-DCMAKE_BUILD_TYPE=Release',
    f'-DBEAM_CUDA_ARCHITECTURES={CUDA_ARCHITECTURES}',
    f'-DCUTLASS_DIR={CUTLASS_DIR}',

import os
from pathlib import Path
import shutil
import subprocess
import sys
import time

def torchrun_world_size():
    nnodes = int(TORCHRUN_NNODES)
    nproc_per_node = int(TORCHRUN_NPROC_PER_NODE)
    if nnodes <= 0 or nproc_per_node <= 0:
        raise ValueError(f'invalid torchrun shape: nnodes={nnodes} nproc_per_node={nproc_per_node}')
    return nnodes * nproc_per_node

def cleanup_path(path):
    path = Path(path)
    if path.exists():
        if path.is_dir():
            shutil.rmtree(path)
        else:
            path.unlink()

def should_print_live(line):
    low = line.lower()
    return (
        'runner_phase=' in line or
        'candidate_history_' in line or
        'history_' in line or
        line.startswith('depth ') or
        'puzzle_' in line or
        'error' in low or
        'exception' in low or
        'terminate called' in low or
        'what():' in low or
        'childfailed' in low or
        'traceback' in low or
        'RUN_' in line
    )

def cfg_get(cfg, key, default):
    return cfg[key] if key in cfg else default

def round_up(value, alignment):
    return ((int(value) + int(alignment) - 1) // int(alignment)) * int(alignment)

def derived_shard_capacity_candidates(cfg):
    world_size = torchrun_world_size()
    shard_count = int(cfg_get(cfg, 'SHARD_COUNT', SHARD_COUNT))
    alignment = int(STREAM4_BATCH_ALIGNMENT)
    beam_alignment = world_size * shard_count * alignment
    global_beam_effective = round_up(BEAM_WIDTH, beam_alignment)
    local_beam_width = global_beam_effective // world_size
    logical_shard_size = (local_beam_width + shard_count - 1) // shard_count
    capacity_raw = (logical_shard_size * int(SHARD_CAPACITY_SCALE_PPM) + 999999) // 1000000
    return round_up(capacity_raw, alignment)

def env_for_config(cfg, puzzle_id):
    model_name = cfg_get(cfg, 'MODEL_TEST', STREAM1_MODEL_TESTS[0]['name'])
    weight_dir = STREAM1_WEIGHT_DIRS[model_name]
    target_weights = REPO_DIR / 'stream1_weights'
    if target_weights.exists() and target_weights.resolve() != Path(weight_dir).resolve():
        shutil.rmtree(target_weights)
    if not target_weights.exists():
        shutil.copytree(weight_dir, target_weights)

    history_path = Path(HISTORY_DISK_PATH) / f'torchrun_p{puzzle_id}_{safe_name(cfg_get(cfg, "name", "config"))}'
    cleanup_path(history_path)
    history_path.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    env = os.environ.copy()
    env.update({
        'BEAM_RUNTIME_CONFIG_MODE': RUNTIME_CONFIG_MODE,
        'BEAM_SHARD_COUNT': str(cfg_get(cfg, 'SHARD_COUNT', SHARD_COUNT)),
        'BEAM_SHARD_CAPACITY_CANDIDATES': str(derived_shard_capacity_candidates(cfg)),
        'BEAM_SHARD_CAPACITY_SCALE_PPM': str(SHARD_CAPACITY_SCALE_PPM),
        'BEAM_SHARD_BUFFER_COUNT': str(SHARD_BUFFER_COUNT),
        'BEAM_GLOBAL_SPILL_CAPACITY': str(GLOBAL_SPILL_CAPACITY),
        'BEAM_STREAM5_RECV_CAPACITY_SCALE_PPM': str(STREAM5_RECV_CAPACITY_SCALE_PPM),
        'BEAM_GPU_HEADROOM_BYTES': str(GPU_HEADROOM_BYTES),
        'BEAM_B_MICRO': str(cfg_get(cfg, 'B_MICRO', B_MICRO)),
        'BEAM_STREAM1_CONCURRENCY': str(cfg_get(cfg, 'STREAM1_CONCURRENCY', STREAM1_CONCURRENCY)),
        'BEAM_STREAM3_RING_SLOTS': str(cfg_get(cfg, 'STREAM3_RING_SLOTS', STREAM3_RING_SLOTS)),
        'BEAM_STREAM4_BATCH_CANDIDATES': str(cfg_get(cfg, 'STREAM4_BATCH_CANDIDATES', STREAM4_BATCH_CANDIDATES)),
        'BEAM_STREAM4_TRIGGER_CANDIDATES': str(cfg_get(cfg, 'STREAM4_TRIGGER_CANDIDATES', STREAM4_TRIGGER_CANDIDATES)),
        'BEAM_STREAM4_ACTIVE_SORT_SLOTS': str(cfg_get(cfg, 'STREAM4_ACTIVE_SORT_SLOTS', STREAM4_ACTIVE_SORT_SLOTS)),
        'BEAM_HISTORY_MODE': str(cfg_get(cfg, 'HISTORY_MODE', HISTORY_MODE)),
        'BEAM_HISTORY_RAM_BYTES': str(HISTORY_RAM_BYTES),
        'BEAM_HISTORY_DISK_BYTES': str(HISTORY_DISK_BYTES),
        'BEAM_HISTORY_DISK_PATH': str(history_path),
        'BEAM_HISTORY_SLOT_COUNT': str(HISTORY_SLOT_COUNT),
        'BEAM_HISTORY_WORKERS': str(HISTORY_WORKERS),
        'BEAM_SOLVED_NEIGHBORHOOD_RADIUS': str(SOLVED_NEIGHBORHOOD_RADIUS),

    })
    env.pop('WORLD_SIZE', None)
    env.pop('RANK', None)
    env.pop('LOCAL_RANK', None)
    return env, history_path

# Project Agent Rules

## Operating Rule
- Read `AGENTS.md` before project edits.
- Update `memory/CHANGELOG.md` after meaningful code, config, or architecture changes.
- Update `memory/PROMPTS.md` with user prompts that define requirements.
- Store test outputs, logs, and verification notes under `test_results/`.
- Prefer agent-centered code: explicit contracts, deterministic tests, small files, low hidden state.

## Architecture Source
- Primary architecture contract: `ARCHITECTURE_NEED.md`.
- Current Stream 4 implementation rule: use threshold + compact + CUB/fixed-temp sort/reduce + compact; do not use Stream 4 hash-table dedup.

## Implementation Guardrails
- `State128` logical bytes are `v[0..119]`.
- `State128` padding bytes are `v[120..127]`.
- Persistent frontier padding must be zero.
- `FinalResponse` may temporarily store `target_local_idx` in `v[120..123]`.
- `Hash128` is one logical 128-bit key stored as `{lo, hi}`.
- `CandidateMeta` must remain 32 bytes and 32-byte aligned.
- Stream 3 payload id must be original candidate id, not compact index.
- Stream 4 must not apply shard top-k or semantic shard cap.
